# Seeded baseline: agent-drafted advisory reviews

Data for [CI-003](code-improvements.md#ci-003--import-the-agent-drafted-baseline-reviews). It holds 2,000 baseline cases and 2,622 alert opinions: 1,205 TP, 1,185 FP, and 232 inconclusive.

The coding agent wrote these opinions on 18 October 2026 from each case's evidence. They passed the backend's schema and citation checks. They are not Gemini output and not human verdicts. Import them with provenance `LOCAL_AGENT_DRAFT_ADVISORY_NOT_HUMAN`, and never count them as Gemini reviews.

- Baseline id: `4b655701f4eda1f479008e0da1d202f48d7fc29beaa41fb95dd31de61ce71de3` (from `python -m backend.seeded_baseline prepare`)
- Reserved cases: none included
- One record per line: `case_id`, `requires_manual_review`, `alert_mapping`, `reviews`
